## File-by-File Purpose Notes

### Root
- package.json: Workspace convenience scripts. start and dev proxy into persev-compiled/backend. start:legacy and dev:legacy proxy into persev-2025-website.

### bss-perseverantia.github.io (legacy simplified copy in this workspace)
- 404.html: Static 404 page.
- index.html, events.html, leaderboard.html, links.html, locations.html, organizing-committee.html: Simplified static pages used during earlier rebuild iteration.
- static/app.js: DOM renderer for simplified cards/nav using SITE_DATA.
- static/site-data.js: Hardcoded data object for simplified pages.
- static/styles.css: Styling for simplified UI.
- assets/placeholder.svg: Placeholder graphic used by simplified copy.
- README.md: Notes for this copy.

### persev-compiled/backend (current backend used by root start/dev)
- index.js: Express server. Serves API routes and frontend static files from ../frontend with extensionless HTML support.
- data/siteData.js: Hardcoded API dataset for site/events/leaderboard.
- package.json: Backend scripts and dependencies.
- package-lock.json: Exact dependency lock.
- LICENSE: Project license text.
- README.md: Backend usage notes.
- public/placeholder.svg: Placeholder static file mounted under /public.
- .gitignore: Git ignore rules for backend folder.

### persev-compiled/frontend (current exact UI copy of hosted site)
- index.html: Home page.
- events.html: Events listing page.
- leaderboard.html: Leaderboard page.
- links.html: Useful links page.
- locations.html: Locations/schedule page.
- organizing-committee.html: Committee page.
- 404.html: 404 page.
- google9e96d9e4f00eeefb.html: Google site verification file.
- robots.txt: Crawl rules.
- sitemap.xml and static/sitemap.xml, static/sitemap(1).xml: Sitemap files.
- config.json: Site content/config consumed by scripts.
- db.json: Data source used by leaderboard logic.
- README.md: Frontend notes.
- static/style.css, static/style.min.css, static/style2.css, static/style_events.css, static/lbstyle.css, static/styles.css: CSS bundles for different pages.
- static/events.js: Main event-page client logic (loading screen, nav injection, modal, animations, link transitions).
- static/confetti.js: Confetti animation engine used by celebratory UI interactions.
- static/app.js and static/site-data.js: Older simplified rendering scripts kept in the tree; not the primary runtime path for live-style pages.
- static/MestizoFont.ttf and assets/MestizoFont.ttf/.woff/.woff2: Custom font files.
- assets/load.mp4, assets/bannerv.mp4, assets/bannerv2.mp4: Video assets.
- assets/handbook.pdf: Downloaded handbook.
- assets/banner*.avif/jpg/jpeg, assets/bannernl.*: Hero/banner images.
- assets/persev*.png/avif and assets/persevlogo.*: Brand logos.
- assets/bronze.jpg, assets/silver.jpg: Medal imagery.
- assets/eventheads/*.avif: Event head photos, one per event.
- assets/eventlogos/*.png and *.avif: Event logo variants, one per event.
- assets/oc2025/<department>/<name>.avif: Organizing committee photos grouped by department.
- assets/text.txt and assets/placeholder.svg: Misc small supporting files.

### persev-2025-website (alternate backend + public mirror)
- index.js: Alternate Express backend, functionally aligned with compiled backend.
- data/siteData.js: Same hardcoded API data model as compiled backend.
- public/*: Mirror of hosted frontend (same purpose as corresponding file in persev-compiled/frontend).
- package.json, package-lock.json, LICENSE, README.md, .gitignore: Standard project metadata and dependency files.

## Function and Variable Explanation (Complete Runtime Symbol Map)

### persev-compiled/backend/index.js
Functions:
- startServer(port): Starts Express on requested port. If port is busy (EADDRINUSE), recursively retries on next port.

Variables:
- express: Express module import.
- path: Node path module import.
- siteData: Imported data object containing site/events/leaderboard.
- app: Express application instance.
- PORT: Initial preferred port from env or 3000.
- frontendDir: Absolute path to served frontend folder.
- registrations: In-memory array storing posted registrations.
- event: Matching event looked up by eventId in register endpoint.
- saved: Registration object persisted in registrations.
- server: Current HTTP server instance from app.listen.
- nextPort: port + 1 used for fallback retry.

Routes and behavior:
- GET /api/health: Health check JSON.
- GET /api/site: Returns siteData.site.
- GET /api/events: Returns siteData.events.
- GET /api/leaderboard: Returns siteData.leaderboard.
- POST /api/register: Validates payload and stores registration.
- GET /api/registrations: Returns in-memory registrations.
- Static middleware: Serves frontendDir with extensions ['html'], so /events resolves to events.html.

### persev-compiled/backend/data/siteData.js
Variables:
- PLACEHOLDER_MEDIA: Shared placeholder path used by dataset entries.
- site: Top-level branding/hero metadata.
- events: Array of event records (id, name, category, description, logo, eventHeadPhoto).
- leaderboard: Event-ended flag plus school points list.

Exports:
- module.exports = { site, events, leaderboard }: Backend API payload source.

### persev-compiled/frontend/static/site-data.js
Variables:
- PLACEHOLDER_IMAGE: Placeholder image path used by simplified static renderer.
- SITE_DATA: Hardcoded client-side content object with title/theme/description/contact/events/committee/leaderboard/quickLinks.

### persev-compiled/frontend/static/app.js
Functions:
- injectNav(): Finds [data-nav] and inserts anchor links from SITE_DATA.quickLinks.
- renderEvents(): Finds [data-events] and renders event cards.
- renderCommittee(): Finds [data-committee] and renders committee cards.
- renderLeaderboard(): Finds [data-leaderboard] and renders table rows.
- injectGlobalContent(): Injects title/theme/description/contact into matching data-* targets.

Variables:
- nav: Navigation mount element.
- mount: Generic mount element used in renderEvents/renderCommittee.
- tbody: Table body mount for leaderboard rows.
- title: Element with [data-site-title].
- theme: Element with [data-theme].
- description: Element with [data-description].
- contact: Element with [data-contact].

### persev-compiled/frontend/static/events.js
Functions:
- initializePage(): Entry loader; fetches config.json, preloads images, builds nav, renders event cards, fades loading screen.
- isObject(variable): Returns true when input is a non-null object and not an array.
- openModal(event): Opens event details modal and binds title/description/head image/ROP link.
- initializeEnhancedAnimations(): Sets observers, ripple effects, and parallax listeners.
- when(...): Helper seen in symbol extraction from minified/transformed page scripts.

Variables (core UI state and DOM handles):
- loadingScreen: Initial loader element.
- res, config, website, navbar, events: Fetched config chain.
- imagePromises, img: Preload pipeline for event media.
- desktopNav, mobileMenu, homeDesktop, homeMobile, desktopLink, mobileLink: Navbar DOM elements.
- grid, card: Event grid and card elements.
- modal, modalTitle, modalDesc, modalImage, eventHeadName, ropLinkBtn, closeModal: Modal subsystem handles.
- toggleBtn, menuOpen: Mobile menu toggle state.
- observerOptions, observer: Intersection observer config + instance.
- ripple, rect, size, x, y, style: Ripple animation values and style node.
- parallax, scrolled: Scroll/parallax targets and offsets.
- targetLink, isInternal, isFile, isMailto, isAnchor, currentLoadingScreen: Link interception logic used to show loading transition during internal navigation.
- eventsDesktop, eventsMobile, ocDesktop, ocMobile: Fallback nav links built when config fetch fails.

### persev-compiled/frontend/static/confetti.js
Functions:
- resetParticle(particle, width, height): Re-seeds one particle with random position/tilt/color.
- toggleConfettiPause(): Switches between paused and running states.
- isConfettiPaused(): Returns pause flag.
- pauseConfetti(): Sets paused state.
- resumeConfetti(): Unpauses and resumes animation loop.
- runAnimation(): Frame loop driver; clears canvas, updates, and draws particles.
- startConfetti(timeout, min, max): Initializes canvas/context and starts particle stream.
- stopConfetti(): Stops adding new particles.
- removeConfetti(): Clears particles and stops animation.
- toggleConfetti(): Starts or stops depending on current state.
- isConfettiRunning(): Returns stream state.
- drawParticles(context): Paints each confetti particle as line segment.
- updateParticles(): Advances particle physics and lifecycle.

Variables:
- confetti: Public config and API object exposed globally.
- supportsAnimationFrame: Browser capability reference.
- colors: Color palette list.
- streamingConfetti: Whether new particles are currently emitted.
- animationTimer: requestAnimationFrame handle.
- pause: Pause-state boolean.
- lastFrameTime: Last frame timestamp for interval pacing.
- particles: Active particle array.
- waveAngle: Shared phase value driving motion.
- context: 2D canvas context.
- canvas, width, height, count, now, delta, particle, i, x, y, x2, y2, gradient, temp: Working variables used in init/draw/update loops.

### Inline script symbols in HTML files

#### index.html
Functions:
- a(): Async bootstrap that fetches config.json, injects nav/content, and handles loading-screen fade-out.
- updateDaysRemaining(): Countdown updater used by hero countdown UI.

Variables:
- queryString, urlParams: URL query parsing.
- day, month, year: Countdown target date values.
- d: Computed date/time helper in countdown logic.
- toggleBtn, menuOpen: Mobile nav toggle controls.
- navTitle: Navbar title element.
- t, e, n, o, c, a: Short minified temporary variables used by compact inline script code.

#### leaderboard.html
Functions:
- fetchAndRender(): Fetches leaderboard data and triggers rendering.
- renderLeaderboard(...): Builds rank table rows and podium blocks.
- createRankBox(...): Creates DOM block for top-rank cards.
- onEventsEnd(...): Branch logic when event has ended.
- openPanel(), closePanel(): Toggles side panel/details UI.

Variables:
- response, data, db, leaderboardData, leaderboard: API/data sources.
- resultContainer, leaderboardBody, podium: Main render mounts.
- schools, grouped, names, maxPoints, points, percentage: Ranking computations.
- positionCell, previousData, previousPosition, previousItem, positionChange, positionArrow: Position delta indicators.
- progressBar, imageWrapper, trophyImages: Visual decoration elements.
- animatedElements, observerOptions, observer, parallax, scrolled: Animation/parallax support.
- start, stop, stoptime, ee: Timing or control flags in animation/update loops.
- podiumCreated: Guard to avoid duplicate podium render.
- barColor, highlightClass, po, pointsa, i, scd, box, existing, row, title, img: Render-time helpers.

#### links.html
Variables:
- loader: Loading overlay element.
- navTitle: Navbar title element.
- rect, ripple, size, x, y: Ripple click effect geometry values.

#### locations.html
Functions:
- loadConfig(): Fetches config/db and populates location schedule tables.

Variables:
- response, data, site: Config/data payload handles.
- searchInput, searchTerm: Search box and current search query.
- allRows, day1VisibleRows, day2VisibleRows, visibleRowsCount: Filter state counters.
- day1Tbody, day2Tbody, noResults: Table and empty-state mounts.
- eventName, location, registrationTime, eventTiming, isMatch: Per-row parsing/filter values.
- loader, mobileMenu, i: UI state and loop index helpers.

#### organizing-committee.html
Functions:
- setupNavbar(): Builds desktop/mobile nav links from config/static links.

Variables:
- res, config, website, navbar: Config fetch chain.
- desktopNav, mobileMenu, homeDesktop, homeMobile, desktopLink, mobileLink: Nav DOM pieces.
- staticLinks: Non-config fallback links.
- toggleBtn, menuOpen: Mobile toggle state.
- loader, loadingScreen: Loading overlay handles.
- targetLink, isInternal, isFile, isMailto, isAnchor: Internal-link interception flags.
- observerOptions, observer, parallax, scrolled: Animation/parallax hooks.
- rect, ripple, size, x, y, style: Ripple animation values and style node.

#### events.html
- No major inline business logic symbols extracted because page logic is primarily delegated to static/events.js.

## Notes on mirrored folders
- Every file under persev-compiled/frontend has a counterpart under persev-2025-website/public with the same relative path and same role.
- Explanations above apply to both locations unless explicitly stated otherwise.

# Humanized Line-by-Line Runtime Explanation

## persev-compiled/backend/index.js

| Line | Code | What This Line Does |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `const express = require("express");` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 2 | `const path = require("path");` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 3 | `const siteData = require("./data/siteData");` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 4 | `` | Spacing line to separate blocks and improve readability. |
| 5 | `const app = express();` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 6 | `const PORT = process.env.PORT &#124;&#124; 3000;` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 7 | `const frontendDir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "frontend");` | Defines a constant binding for config, module import, or computed value. |
| 8 | `` | Spacing line to separate blocks and improve readability. |
| 9 | `app.use(express.json());` | Registers Express middleware in request-processing order. |
| 10 | `app.use("/public", express.static(path.join(__dirname, "public")));` | Registers Express middleware in request-processing order. |
| 11 | `app.use(express.static(frontendDir, { extensions: ["html"] }));` | Registers Express middleware in request-processing order. |
| 12 | `` | Spacing line to separate blocks and improve readability. |
| 13 | `// In-memory registrations for demo simplicity.` | Comment that documents intent for the following code block. |
